87:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["Scalus’s JavaScript build runs the Plutus conformance suite in CI, with the corpus of the\nplutus-core version that the latest production cardano-node runs: ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"every"}]," UPLC evaluation case,\nin both its textual and flat-encoded form, none skipped. For each program the reference evaluates\nsuccessfully, both the resulting term and the exact CPU and memory budget are asserted against the\nreference implementation.
